`tests/test_config.py`:

```py
from pathlib import Path
from shutil import which
from sys import executable

import pytest
from typer.testing import CliRunner

from snare.cli import app
from snare.config import load_config

EXAMPLE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "snare.example.toml"


def test_example_is_valid() -> None:
    settings = load_config(EXAMPLE)
    assert settings.network.default_mode == "cut"
    assert settings.paths.runs == "runs"


def test_config_check_with_tools_available(
    tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    config = tmp_path / "snare.toml"
    sample = EXAMPLE.read_text(encoding="utf-8").split("[tools]", 1)[0]
    command = Path(executable).name
    monkeypatch.setenv("PATH", str(Path(executable).parent))
    assert which(command) is not None
    config.write_text(
        sample
        + f"""[tools]
volatility = "{command}"
memprocfs = "{command}"
zeek = "{command}"
suricata = "{command}"
""",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    result = CliRunner().invoke(app, ["config", "check", "--config", str(config)])
    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
    assert "All configured external tools are available." in result.output


def test_config_check_lists_missing_tools(
    tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setenv("PATH", str(tmp_path))
    # Avoid picking up an executable from the caller's current directory on Windows.
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    config = tmp_path / "snare.toml"
    config.write_bytes(EXAMPLE.read_bytes())

    result = CliRunner().invoke(app, ["config", "check"])

    assert result.exit_code == 1
    assert "Configuration valid" in result.stdout
    for name in ("volatility", "memprocfs", "zeek", "suricata"):
        assert f"tools.{name}" in result.stderr


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("content", "expected"),
    [
        ("[broken", "Configuration error"),
        ("_cli_parse_args = true", "Unknown top-level setting: _cli_parse_args"),
        (
            EXAMPLE.read_text(encoding="utf-8").replace('"cut"', '"invalid"'),
            "network.default_mode",
        ),
    ],
)
def test_config_check_rejects_invalid_files(
    content: str, expected: str, tmp_path: Path
) -> None:
    config = tmp_path / "invalid.toml"
    config.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")

    result = CliRunner().invoke(app, ["config", "check", "-c", str(config)])

    assert result.exit_code == 1
    assert expected in result.stderr


def test_config_check_reports_missing_file(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    config = tmp_path / "missing.toml"

    result = CliRunner().invoke(app, ["config", "check", "-c", str(config)])

    assert result.exit_code == 1
    assert "missing.toml" in result.stderr

```

`tests/test_models.py`:

```py
import json
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from jsonschema import Draft202012Validator, FormatChecker
from pydantic import ValidationError
from typer.testing import CliRunner

from snare.cli import app
from snare.models import Event, Finding, Marker, Run

PROJECT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
TIME = "2026-11-10T18:02:16Z"


@pytest.fixture
def finding_payload() -> dict[str, object]:
    readme = (PROJECT / "README.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    section = readme.split("## Example finding (JSON)", 1)[1]
    payload = json.loads(section.split("```json", 1)[1].split("```", 1)[0])
    assert isinstance(payload, dict)
    return dict(payload)


def test_finding_round_trip(finding_payload: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    finding = Finding.model_validate(finding_payload)
    assert Finding.model_validate_json(finding.model_dump_json()) == finding


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("field", "value"),
    [
        ("confidence", 1.01),
        ("type", "undotted"),
        ("evidence", ["../outside.bin"]),
        ("evidence", ["C:/outside.bin"]),
    ],
)
def test_invalid_finding(
    finding_payload: dict[str, object], field: str, value: object
) -> None:
    finding_payload[field] = value
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match=field):
        Finding.model_validate(finding_payload)


def test_marker_and_event_round_trip() -> None:
    marker = Marker.model_validate(
        {"t_ns": 10, "wall_time": TIME, "text": "Demo started"}
    )
    assert Marker.model_validate_json(marker.model_dump_json()) == marker

    event = marker.to_event()
    assert event.source == "analyst"
    assert event.data == {"text": "Demo started"}
    assert Event.model_validate_json(event.model_dump_json()) == event


def test_run_round_trip() -> None:
    run = Run.model_validate(
        {
            "run_id": "20261110-180216-baseline-a1b2c3d4",
            "profile": "demo",
            "mode": "baseline",
            "network": "cut",
            "started_at": TIME,
            "snare_version": "0.1.0",
        }
    )
    assert Run.model_validate_json(run.model_dump_json()) == run


def test_exported_schemas(tmp_path: Path, finding_payload: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    result = CliRunner().invoke(
        app, ["schema", "export", "--output-dir", str(tmp_path)]
    )
    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output

    event = Marker.model_validate(
        {"t_ns": 10, "wall_time": TIME, "text": "Ready"}
    ).to_event()
    for name, payload in (
        ("finding", finding_payload),
        ("event", event.model_dump(mode="json")),
    ):
        filename = f"{name}.schema.json"
        generated = (tmp_path / filename).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        assert generated == (PROJECT / "schemas" / filename).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        schema = json.loads(generated)
        Draft202012Validator.check_schema(schema)
        Draft202012Validator(schema, format_checker=FormatChecker()).validate(payload)

```
